## Task 7: L0 wrap-up and the maintainer's decision (CP-L0)

**Goal:** one short summary for the maintainer of what L-a still needs from L0 (the baseline, Task 2, and the tray spike, Task 4; L0-1 and L0-3 are done and already in the spec; L0-4 is deferred and not needed), the spec updated where the tray findings change decision 4 (only after the maintainer agrees), and Task 17's parameters fixed.

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-29-linvpn-design.md` (only with the maintainer's agreement; a dated note per change, as the WinVPN spec's "Note (2c)")
- Modify: this plan's Task 17 header (fill in `TRAY` and `FORCE_X11`)

**Done 2026-10-29 (CP-L0).** The maintainer's decisions:
- **Tray:** `TRAY = S`, own StatusNotifierItem over `ksni` 0.3.7. A single left or right click shows a short menu whose first item is "Open LinVPN"; a double click (`Activate`) or a middle click (`SecondaryActivate`) toggles the panel. Checked by the maintainer on screen: the double click shows the card at the top-right under the top bar; a click outside hides it. The single-click menu delay (the extension's 400 ms double-click wait) is acceptable.
- **Panel:** the transparent maximized overlay window on Wayland, input region = the card, re-applied on `size-allocate`. `FORCE_X11 = false`; Xwayland only as the fallback if the overlay fails.
- **Decision 4 stands** (its menu is the single-click path on GNOME). Spec note under decision 4.
- **WebKit without a GPU:** the VM has no 3D acceleration; WebKit logs libEGL/MESA warnings, harmless. Task 18 decides whether to set `WEBKIT_DISABLE_DMABUF_RENDERER=1` when there is no render node (Task 18's CP-L0 amendments).
- **L0-4** (static and CRV1 challenge) stays deferred until the maintainer starts the VPN lab (Task 6; spec note in section 4, MFA).
- **Open for L-b:** a `redirect-gateway` pushed by the server must be honoured automatically (maintainer requirement, 2026-10-29); the research runs on a separate branch; decision 7 / spec section 4 are open on that point (spec note).
- The baseline (Task 2) is in `docs/superpowers/spikes/2026-10-29-linvpn-baseline.md`; Task 11 works from it.

# Part 2 — L-a: foundations

## Task 8: LinVPN's product identity and its update signature

**Goal:** `LINVPN` exists in platform-api with its update assets; the signed message `linvpn-update-v1\n<asset>\n<version>\n<sha256>\n` binds the product, the architecture and the version; a signature of one product or architecture never verifies for another; `scripts/sign-release.sh` signs the two `.deb`s ("both or neither"). Pure: Mac only, no VM.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/src/update.rs` (the "MARK: - Signature" section), `crates/core/tests/update.rs`
- Modify: `crates/platform-api/src/product.rs`, `crates/platform-api/src/lib.rs`, `crates/platform-api/tests/product.rs`
- Modify: `scripts/sign-release.sh`, `scripts/test-sign-release.sh`

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing core tests** — append to `crates/core/tests/update.rs` (after `winvpn_signature_is_bound_to_version_and_hash`):

```rust
const AMD64: &str = "linvpn_0.2.2_amd64.deb";
const DEB_ARM64: &str = "linvpn_0.2.2_arm64.deb";

#[test]
fn linvpn_message_names_the_asset() {
    assert_eq!(
        UpdateSignature::message_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", DIGEST),
        Some(format!("linvpn-update-v1\n{AMD64}\n0.2.2\n{DIGEST}\n").into_bytes())
    );
    assert_eq!(
        UpdateSignature::message_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, DEB_ARM64, "0.2.2", DIGEST),
        Some(format!("linvpn-update-v1\n{DEB_ARM64}\n0.2.2\n{DIGEST}\n").into_bytes())
    );
}

#[test]
fn linvpn_message_refuses_any_other_asset_name() {
    for asset in [
        "",
        "MacVPN-0.2.2.dmg",
        X64,
        "linvpn_0.2.3_amd64.deb",
        "linvpn_0.2.2_i386.deb",
        "LinVPN_0.2.2_amd64.deb",
        "linvpn-0.2.2-amd64.deb",
        "linvpn_0.2.2_amd64.deb\n",
        "linvpn_0.2.2_amd64.DEB",
        "../linvpn_0.2.2_amd64.deb",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(UpdateSignature::message_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, asset, "0.2.2", DIGEST), None, "{asset:?}");
    }
    assert_eq!(UpdateSignature::message_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2", DIGEST), None);
    assert_eq!(UpdateSignature::message_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", &DIGEST[..63]), None);
}

#[test]
fn no_product_accepts_another_products_signature() {
    let lin = sign_for(&key(1), SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", DIGEST);
    assert!(UpdateSignature::verify_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", DIGEST, &lin, &keys()));
    assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify("0.2.2", DIGEST, &lin, &keys()));
    assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify_for(SignatureDomain::WinVpnV1, X64, "0.2.2", DIGEST, &lin, &keys()));
    let win = sign_for(&key(1), SignatureDomain::WinVpnV1, X64, "0.2.2", DIGEST);
    let mac = sign(&key(1), "0.2.2", DIGEST);
    for sig in [&win, &mac] {
        assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", DIGEST, sig, &keys()));
    }
}

#[test]
fn linvpn_rejects_the_amd64_signature_for_the_arm64_asset() {
    let sig = sign_for(&key(2), SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", DIGEST);
    assert!(UpdateSignature::verify_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", DIGEST, &sig, &keys()));
    assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, DEB_ARM64, "0.2.2", DIGEST, &sig, &keys()));
    let other = format!("{}0", &DIGEST[..63]);
    assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify_for(SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1, AMD64, "0.2.2", &other, &sig, &keys()));
}
```

```bash
if [ "$DRY_RUN" = 1 ]; then
  [ "$REPUBLISH" = 0 ] || die "--republish-sig cannot be combined with --dry-run"
  CHOSEN=0
  for f in "$DRY_DMG" "$DRY_MSI" "$DRY_DEB"; do [ -z "$f" ] || CHOSEN=$((CHOSEN + 1)); done
  [ "$CHOSEN" = 1 ] || die "--dry-run needs exactly one of --dmg FILE, --msi FILE --arch ARCH, --deb FILE --arch ARCH"
  if [ -n "$DRY_DMG" ]; then
    [ -z "$DRY_ARCH" ] || die "--arch is only for --msi and --deb"
    [ -f "$DRY_DMG" ] || die "not a file: $DRY_DMG"
    DRY_FILE="$DRY_DMG"; DRY_ASSET="$DMG_NAME"
  elif [ -n "$DRY_MSI" ]; then

    DRY_FILE="$DRY_DEB"; DRY_ASSET="linvpn_${VERSION}_${DRY_ARCH}.deb"
  fi
else
  [ -z "$DRY_DMG$DRY_MSI$DRY_DEB$DRY_ARCH" ] || die "--dmg, --msi, --deb and --arch are only for --dry-run"
fi
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The Rust-side check of the script** — add to `crates/core/tests/update.rs` after `sign_release_dry_run_msi_matches_app` (copy its body; differences only):

```rust
/// `scripts/sign-release.sh --dry-run --deb FILE --arch arm64` with a throwaway key: the signature verifies in
/// LinVPN's domain for the arm64 package only.
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn sign_release_dry_run_deb_matches_app() {
    let Some(openssl) = openssl3() else {
        eprintln!("skipped: OpenSSL 3 not installed");
        return;
    };
    let tmp = TempDir::new();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(tmp.path()).unwrap();
    let (pem, public) = openssl_key(openssl, tmp.path());
    let deb = tmp.path().join("built.deb");
    std::fs::write(&deb, (0..7000u32).map(|i| (i % 239) as u8).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap();
    let out = Command::new("/bin/bash")
        .arg(repo_root().join("scripts/sign-release.sh"))
        .args(["--dry-run", "--deb"])
        .arg(&deb)
        .args(["--arch", "arm64", "--backup-key"])
        .arg(&pem)
        .arg("v0.2.2")
        .env("HTTPS_PROXY", "http://127.0.0.1:9")
        .env("GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT", "0")
        .stdin(std::process::Stdio::null())
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{stdout}");
    let line = stdout.lines().find(|l| l.starts_with("signature: ")).unwrap();
    let sig = format!("{}\n", &line["signature: ".len()..]);
    let local = sha256_of_file(&deb).unwrap();
    let lin = SignatureDomain::LinVpnV1;
    assert!(UpdateSignature::verify_for(lin, "linvpn_0.2.2_arm64.deb", "0.2.2", &local, &sig, &[public]));
    assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify_for(lin, "linvpn_0.2.2_amd64.deb", "0.2.2", &local, &sig, &[public]));
    assert!(!UpdateSignature::verify("0.2.2", &local, &sig, &[public]));
}
```

(Use the same `#[cfg(...)]` attribute the MSI test has, if it has one other than `unix`.)

```rust
//! The Unix system calls macOS and Linux share (LinVPN plan, "Resolved points"): the kernel's randomness
//! ([`random`]) and, from Task 10, the control API's Unix socket. Unix only: on Windows the crate is empty.
//!
//! `unsafe` is confined to direct libc calls with plain arguments or stack buffers whose length is passed alongside,
//! each with a `SAFETY` comment; the public API is safe.
```

`crates/unixsys/src/random.rs`:

```rust
//! The kernel's CSPRNG: `getentropy` on macOS (256 bytes per call), `getrandom(2)` on Linux (retried on `EINTR` and
//! short reads).

use std::io;

/// Fills `buf` from the kernel's CSPRNG.
pub fn fill(buf: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
    imp::fill(buf)
}

/// 32 random bytes (tokens, keys).
pub fn random_32() -> io::Result<[u8; 32]> {
    let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
    fill(&mut bytes)?;
    Ok(bytes)
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
mod imp {
    use std::io;

    pub fn fill(buf: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
        for chunk in buf.chunks_mut(256) {
            // SAFETY: `chunk` is writable for its length (at most 256, getentropy's limit), passed alongside.
            if unsafe { libc::getentropy(chunk.as_mut_ptr().cast(), chunk.len()) } != 0 {
                return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod imp {
    use std::io;

    pub fn fill(buf: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut filled = 0;
        while filled < buf.len() {
            let rest = &mut buf[filled..];
            // SAFETY: `rest` is writable for its length, passed alongside; flags 0 = the urandom pool, blocking
            // only until it is initialized at boot.
            let n = unsafe { libc::getrandom(rest.as_mut_ptr().cast(), rest.len(), 0) };
            if n < 0 {
                let e = io::Error::last_os_error();
                if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted {
                    continue;
                }
                return Err(e);
            }
            filled += n as usize;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn fills_large_buffers_and_differs() {
    let mut a = vec![0u8; 1000];
    let mut b = vec![0u8; 1000];
    random::fill(&mut a).unwrap();
    random::fill(&mut b).unwrap();
    assert_ne!(a, b);
    assert!(a.iter().any(|&x| x != 0));
    assert_ne!(random::random_32().unwrap(), random::random_32().unwrap());
}
```

```rust
/// Whether `peer_uid` is this process's real uid.
pub fn same_user(peer_uid: u32) -> bool {
    // SAFETY: `getuid` takes no argument and cannot fail.
    peer_uid == unsafe { libc::getuid() }
}
```

4. `SUN_PATH_LEN` per OS:

```rust
/// `sizeof(((struct sockaddr_un *)0)->sun_path)`: 104 on macOS (`<sys/un.h>`), 108 on Linux. The path plus its
/// NUL terminator must fit.
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
const SUN_PATH_LEN: usize = 104;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
const SUN_PATH_LEN: usize = 108;
```

5. Put `#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]` on the existing `peer` and `executable_path` (unchanged), and add the Linux pair after them:

```rust
/// The peer's identity on Linux: `getsockopt(SOL_SOCKET, SO_PEERCRED)` (the credentials the peer had when it
/// connected; the kernel records them at `connect`, so they cannot be changed later) and its executable from
/// `/proc/<pid>/exe`.
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub fn peer(stream: &UnixStream) -> io::Result<Peer> {
    let fd = stream.as_raw_fd();
    let mut cred = libc::ucred { pid: 0, uid: 0, gid: 0 };
    let mut length = std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() as libc::socklen_t;
    // SAFETY: the out-buffer is `cred` on the stack and its size is passed in `length`.
    let rc = unsafe { libc::getsockopt(fd, libc::SOL_SOCKET, libc::SO_PEERCRED, (&raw mut cred).cast(), &mut length) };
    if rc != 0 {
        return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
    }
    if length as usize != std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() {
        return Err(error(ErrorKind::InvalidData, format!("SO_PEERCRED returned {length} bytes")));
    }
    Ok(Peer { uid: cred.uid, pid: cred.pid, executable: executable_path(cred.pid) })
}

/// `/proc/<pid>/exe`'s target; `None` if it cannot be read, or the file was replaced or deleted since the process
/// started (the kernel appends ` (deleted)`): such a peer cannot pair or authenticate.
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn executable_path(pid: i32) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if pid <= 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let target = fs::read_link(format!("/proc/{pid}/exe")).ok()?;
    if target.as_os_str().as_bytes().ends_with(b" (deleted)") {
        return None;
    }
    Some(target)
}
```

Keep every macOS sentence. `Peer::pid`'s doc: "macOS: `LOCAL_PEERPID` (…); Linux: `SO_PEERCRED`'s pid at connect time."

```rust
fn bind_at_uses_the_given_names() {
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: The listener and stream wrappers move too** — `crates/platform-mac/src/api/control.rs`'s `UnixControlListener` and `UnixControlStream` (and their trait impls) are portable Unix code: move them into `crates/unixsys/src/transport.rs` (add `macvpn-platform-api = { path = "../platform-api" }` to unixsys' `[target.'cfg(unix)'.dependencies]`, `pub mod transport;` to its `lib.rs`), with one new helper the two platforms share:

```rust
/// The bound socket as platform-api's endpoint: the non-blocking listener, the path as the log line shows it, the
/// socket file the server removes at stop, and the lock as the guard it drops last.
pub fn endpoint(bound: crate::control_socket::ControlListener, path: PathBuf) -> io::Result<BoundEndpoint> {
    let listener = UnixControlListener::new(bound.listener().try_clone()?)?;
    Ok(BoundEndpoint {
        listener: Box::new(listener),
        display: path.display().to_string(),
        socket_file: Some(path),
        guard: Box::new(bound),
    })
}
```

```rust
/// Linux: the peer of a socket this process connected to itself is this process: its uid, its pid and its
/// executable (the test binary).
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
fn linux_peer_is_this_process() {
    use std::os::unix::net::UnixStream;
    let (a, _b) = UnixStream::pair().unwrap();
    let peer = macvpn_unixsys::control_socket::peer(&a).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(peer.pid, std::process::id() as i32);
    assert!(macvpn_unixsys::control_socket::same_user(peer.uid));
    assert_eq!(peer.executable.unwrap(), std::env::current_exe().unwrap());
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `pure/paths.rs`**

```rust
//! The XDG base directories LinVPN uses (spec 3.2): data `$XDG_DATA_HOME/LinVPN` (default `~/.local/share`),
//! settings `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/LinVPN` (default `~/.config`), the update cache `$XDG_CACHE_HOME/LinVPN/updates`
//! (default `~/.cache`), run files `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn`. A variable counts only when it is set, non-empty and
//! absolute (the XDG Base Directory spec: a relative value is invalid and ignored). The sandbox keeps all of them
//! under its home, with `<sandbox>/run` as the runtime dir. The data dir's name comes from `Product::data_dir`; the
//! other names are storage identifiers (literals).

use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Xdg {
    pub home: PathBuf,
    pub data_home: PathBuf,
    pub config_home: PathBuf,
    pub cache_home: PathBuf,
    /// `None`: unset or invalid (no control socket then: fail closed).
    pub runtime_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
}

fn absolute(value: Option<OsString>) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    value.map(PathBuf::from).filter(|p| p.is_absolute())
}

impl Xdg {
    pub fn from_env(home: &Path, var: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<OsString>) -> Xdg {
        Xdg {
            home: home.to_path_buf(),
            data_home: absolute(var("XDG_DATA_HOME")).unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".local/share")),
            config_home: absolute(var("XDG_CONFIG_HOME")).unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".config")),
            cache_home: absolute(var("XDG_CACHE_HOME")).unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".cache")),
            runtime_dir: absolute(var("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")),
        }
    }

    pub fn sandbox(root: &Path) -> Xdg {
        Xdg {
            home: root.to_path_buf(),
            data_home: root.join(".local/share"),
            config_home: root.join(".config"),
            cache_home: root.join(".cache"),
            runtime_dir: Some(root.join("run")),
        }
    }

    /// `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/LinVPN/preferences.json`.
    pub fn preferences_file(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.config_home.join("LinVPN").join("preferences.json")
    }

    /// `$XDG_CACHE_HOME/LinVPN/updates` (private, 0700).
    pub fn updates_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.cache_home.join("LinVPN").join("updates")
    }

    /// `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/autostart/linvpn.desktop`.
    pub fn autostart_file(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.config_home.join("autostart").join("linvpn.desktop")
    }

    /// `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn`.
    pub fn runtime(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.runtime_dir.as_ref().map(|r| r.join("linvpn"))
    }

    /// `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock`.
    pub fn control_socket(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.runtime().map(|r| r.join("control").join("linvpn.sock"))
    }
}
```

```rust
use macvpn_platform_linux::pure::languages::preferred_languages;
use std::collections::HashMap;

fn langs(pairs: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Vec<String> {
    let map: HashMap<&str, &str> = pairs.iter().copied().collect();
    preferred_languages(|k| map.get(k).map(|v| v.to_string()))
}

#[test]
fn language_list_first_then_the_locale_variables() {
    assert_eq!(langs(&[("LANGUAGE", "hu:en_GB:en"), ("LANG", "de_DE.UTF-8")]), ["hu", "en-GB", "en"]);
    assert_eq!(langs(&[("LC_ALL", "hu_HU.UTF-8"), ("LANG", "en_US.UTF-8")]), ["hu-HU"]);
    assert_eq!(langs(&[("LC_MESSAGES", "hu_HU@euro"), ("LANG", "en_US.UTF-8")]), ["hu-HU"]);
    assert_eq!(langs(&[("LANG", "en_US.UTF-8")]), ["en-US"]);
}

#[test]
fn c_posix_and_empty_mean_nothing() {
    assert!(langs(&[("LANG", "C.UTF-8")]).is_empty());
    assert!(langs(&[("LANG", "POSIX")]).is_empty());
    assert!(langs(&[("LANGUAGE", ""), ("LANG", "")]).is_empty());
    // LANGUAGE is ignored when the locale is C (gettext's rule).
    assert!(langs(&[("LANGUAGE", "hu"), ("LC_ALL", "C")]).is_empty());
}
```

`crates/platform-linux/src/pure/languages.rs`:

```rust
//! The user's preferred languages from the environment, in gettext's order: `LANGUAGE` (a colon list; ignored when
//! the locale is `C`/`POSIX`), then the first set of `LC_ALL`, `LC_MESSAGES`, `LANG`. `hu_HU.UTF-8@euro` becomes
//! `hu-HU` (the BCP 47 form `AppLanguage::resolve` reads).

fn tag(locale: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let base = locale.split(['.', '@']).next().unwrap_or("");
    if base.is_empty() || base == "C" || base == "POSIX" {
        return None;
    }
    Some(base.replace('_', "-"))
}

pub fn preferred_languages(var: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Vec<String> {
    let set = |k: &str| var(k).filter(|v| !v.is_empty());
    let locale = set("LC_ALL").or_else(|| set("LC_MESSAGES")).or_else(|| set("LANG"));
    let Some(primary) = locale.as_deref().and_then(tag) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    if let Some(list) = set("LANGUAGE") {
        let tags: Vec<String> = list.split(':').filter_map(tag).collect();
        if !tags.is_empty() {
            return tags;
        }
    }
    vec![primary]
}
```

(`LANGUAGE` without any locale variable: gettext ignores it too; the test `c_posix_and_empty_mean_nothing` covers the `C` case.) Add `pub mod languages;`. Run → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 5: The D-Bus connections** — `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/bus.rs`:

```rust
//! One session-bus and one system-bus connection per process, opened on first use (never at construction: the
//! sandbox and the tests open none unless they call a D-Bus service). `zbus::blocking::Connection` is cheap to
//! clone (shared).

use std::sync::{Mutex, PoisonError};
use zbus::blocking::Connection;

static SESSION: Mutex<Option<Connection>> = Mutex::new(None);
static SYSTEM: Mutex<Option<Connection>> = Mutex::new(None);

fn get(slot: &Mutex<Option<Connection>>, open: fn() -> zbus::Result<Connection>) -> zbus::Result<Connection> {
    let mut guard = slot.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner);
    if let Some(c) = guard.as_ref() {
        return Ok(c.clone());
    }
    let c = open()?;
    *guard = Some(c.clone());
    Ok(c)
}

pub fn session() -> zbus::Result<Connection> {
    get(&SESSION, Connection::session)
}

pub fn system() -> zbus::Result<Connection> {
    get(&SYSTEM, Connection::system)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The system facts** — `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/system.rs`:

```rust
//! The user from the user database (`getpwuid_r`, not `$HOME`), the local time (`localtime_r`).

use std::ffi::{CStr, OsStr};
use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
use std::path::PathBuf;

fn passwd_field(field: fn(&libc::passwd) -> *const libc::c_char) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; 16 * 1024];
    let mut pwd: libc::passwd = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    let mut result: *mut libc::passwd = std::ptr::null_mut();
    // SAFETY: `pwd`, `buf` and `result` live on this stack frame for the call; `buf`'s length is passed.
    let rc = unsafe { libc::getpwuid_r(libc::getuid(), &mut pwd, buf.as_mut_ptr().cast(), buf.len(), &mut result) };
    if rc != 0 || result.is_null() {
        return None;
    }
    let p = field(&pwd);
    if p.is_null() {
        return None;
    }
    // SAFETY: `p` points into `buf`, NUL-terminated by getpwuid_r, valid while `buf` lives.
    Some(unsafe { CStr::from_ptr(p) }.to_bytes().to_vec())
}

/// The home directory of the user running the app.
pub fn home() -> Option<PathBuf> {
    passwd_field(|p| p.pw_dir).map(|b| PathBuf::from(OsStr::from_bytes(&b))).filter(|p| p.is_absolute())
}

/// The login name.
pub fn user_name() -> Option<String> {
    passwd_field(|p| p.pw_name).and_then(|b| String::from_utf8(b).ok())
}

/// `(year, month, day, hour, minute, second)` in local time; `None` if it cannot be converted.
pub fn local_time(unix: f64) -> Option<(i32, u32, u32, u32, u32, u32)> {
    if !unix.is_finite() {
        return None;
    }
    let t = unix.floor() as libc::time_t;
    let mut tm: libc::tm = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    // SAFETY: `t` and `tm` live on this stack frame for the call.
    if unsafe { libc::localtime_r(&t, &mut tm) }.is_null() {
        return None;
    }
    Some((tm.tm_year + 1900, (tm.tm_mon + 1) as u32, tm.tm_mday as u32, tm.tm_hour as u32, tm.tm_min as u32, tm.tm_sec as u32))
}
```

(The `std::mem::zeroed()` calls are `unsafe` too: wrap each in `// SAFETY: a plain C struct of integers and pointers; all-zero is a valid value.`.) Check the tuple shape against `macvpn_platform_api::dates::{date, date_time}` and `pure::ras_connect::timestamp` in platform-win (the Windows `system::local_time` returns the same shape); adapt the tuple to it.

- [ ] **Step 7: The shell** — `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/shell.rs`:

```rust
//! Opening folders and URLs with the desktop's handler (`/usr/bin/xdg-open`, which GNOME routes to `gio open`),
//! detached; never a file (that would run or open it with an unknown app).

use macvpn_platform_api::PlatformError;
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};

const XDG_OPEN: &str = "/usr/bin/xdg-open";

fn spawn(argument: &std::ffi::OsStr) -> Result<(), PlatformError> {
    Command::new(XDG_OPEN)
        .arg(argument)
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(Stdio::null())
        .stderr(Stdio::null())
        .spawn()
        .map(|_| ())
        .map_err(|e| PlatformError::Failed(format!("{XDG_OPEN}: {e}")))
}

/// Absolute directories only.
pub fn open_directory(path: &Path) -> Result<(), PlatformError> {
    if !path.is_absolute() || !path.is_dir() {
        return Err(PlatformError::Failed("not an absolute directory".into()));
    }
    spawn(path.as_os_str())
}

/// `https://` only (the callers validate the URL first: `is_safe_github_url`).
pub fn open_https(url: &str) -> Result<(), PlatformError> {
    if !url.starts_with("https://") {
        return Err(PlatformError::Failed("refused URL".into()));
    }
    spawn(std::ffi::OsStr::new(url))
}
```

(`xdg-open` takes one argument and no options; a URL starting with `https://` cannot look like one.)

- [ ] **Step 9: `lin/platform.rs`** — model it on `crates/platform-win/src/win/platform.rs` (read it first): the same accessor order, an `Inert` type for the stand-ins, `SystemEntropy`, the sandbox's memory stores. The Linux-specific parts:

```rust
//! `LinPlatform`: every `platform-api` trait for Linux. L-a's stand-ins (`Inert`) are replaced by the later tasks,
//! each in its own accessor: the key store (Task 13), the control transport (14), the notifier, file panels,
//! clipboard, window chrome and download mark (15), the lifecycle and login items (16), the release source, HTTPS
//! catalogs and updater (20).

use crate::lin::{shell, system};
use crate::pure::paths::Xdg;
use crate::pure::{languages, texts};
use macvpn_core::l10n::{self, AppLanguage};
use macvpn_core::vault::{Entropy, KeyStore, LegacyKeychain, MemoryKeyStore};
use macvpn_platform_api::*;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::{Arc, OnceLock};

struct Sandbox {
    key_store: Arc<MemoryKeyStore>,
    preferences: Arc<MemoryPreferences>,
}

pub struct LinPlatform {
    sandbox: Option<Sandbox>,
    preferences: OnceLock<Arc<FilePreferences>>,
}

impl LinPlatform {
    /// `sandbox` (debug builds, `MACVPN_SANDBOX_HOME`): memory key store and preferences, data under the sandbox
    /// home; never the Secret Service, never the real XDG directories, no D-Bus at construction.
    pub fn new(sandbox: bool) -> LinPlatform {
        LinPlatform {
            sandbox: sandbox.then(|| Sandbox {
                key_store: Arc::new(MemoryKeyStore::new()),
                preferences: Arc::new(MemoryPreferences::new()),
            }),
            preferences: OnceLock::new(),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn is_sandbox(&self) -> bool {
        self.sandbox.is_some()
    }

    /// The XDG directories of the real user (the sandbox's come from its home, which the caller passes).
    pub(crate) fn xdg(&self, home: &Path) -> Xdg {
        if self.is_sandbox() {
            Xdg::sandbox(home)
        } else {
            Xdg::from_env(home, |k| std::env::var_os(k))
        }
    }
}

struct SystemEntropy;

impl Entropy for SystemEntropy {
    fn fill(&self, buf: &mut [u8]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        macvpn_unixsys::random::fill(buf)
    }
}
```

The accessors (only the Linux-specific bodies are shown; everything else as `WinPlatform`'s `Inert` answers):

```rust
impl Platform for LinPlatform {
    fn product(&self) -> &'static Product {
        &LINVPN
    }

    /// The preferred languages from the environment and the text platform. Collation: the core's built-in.
    fn install_core_hooks(&self) {
        AppLanguage::set_preferred_languages(languages::preferred_languages(|k| std::env::var(k).ok()));
        l10n::set_text_platform(LINVPN.text_platform());
    }

    fn key_store(&self) -> Arc<dyn KeyStore> {
        match &self.sandbox {
            Some(s) => s.key_store.clone(),
            // Task 13: the Secret Service.
            None => Arc::new(MemoryKeyStore::new()),
        }
    }

    fn entropy(&self) -> Arc<dyn Entropy> {
        Arc::new(SystemEntropy)
    }

    /// `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/LinVPN/preferences.json` (private directory and file); the sandbox's are in memory.
    fn preferences(&self) -> Arc<dyn PreferenceStore> {
        if let Some(s) = &self.sandbox {
            return s.preferences.clone();
        }
        self.preferences
            .get_or_init(|| {
                let home = system::home().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/nonexistent"));
                Arc::new(FilePreferences::new(Xdg::from_env(&home, |k| std::env::var_os(k)).preferences_file()))
            })
            .clone()
    }

    fn helper_client(&self) -> Arc<dyn HelperClient> {
        Arc::new(NotYet)
    }

    fn native_controller(&self) -> Arc<dyn NativeController> {
        Arc::new(NotYet)
    }

    fn native_profiles(&self, _staging: &Path, _open: bool) -> Arc<dyn NativeProfiles> {
        Arc::new(NotYet)
    }

    // … every other accessor: `self` for SystemInfo/Shell/SystemSetup/DownloadMark/SystemVpnWatch, `&Inert` or
    // `Arc::new(Inert)` for the rest, exactly as WinPlatform's 2a stand-ins (FilePanels: `done(vec![])` at once;
    // Notifier: nothing; SecretClipboard: `Err(Unsupported)`; AppLifecycle: `false`; LoginItems: the no-op item;
    // ControlTransport: binds nothing, `Unsupported`; ReleaseSource / CatalogFetcher / Updater: `Unsupported` /
    // `Err(not_yet())`; LegacyKeychain / LegacySecrets: empty; AuthStateReader: `NoAuthState`; LogStreamSpawner:
    // unavailable). `migrate_legacy_service`: nothing.
}
```

`FilePreferences::new` must create the parent with mode 0700: check `crates/platform-api/src/prefs.rs` (it uses `fsx`); if it only writes the file, create the directory with `macvpn_core::fsx::create_private_dir_all` before constructing it.

`NotYet` (the L-a engines):

```rust
/// L-a's engines: nothing runs, every start answers [`texts::not_yet`].
struct NotYet;

impl HelperClient for NotYet {
    fn status(&self) -> Result<Vec<macvpn_core::status::RootStatus>, String> {
        Ok(Vec::new())
    }
    fn up(&self, _: macvpn_core::models::VPNKind, _: &uuid::Uuid, _: &[u8], _: &macvpn_core::models::Secrets, _: bool, _: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
        Err(texts::not_yet())
    }
    fn down(&self, _: macvpn_core::models::VPNKind, _: &uuid::Uuid) -> Result<(), String> {
        Err(texts::not_yet())
    }
    fn respond(&self, _: &uuid::Uuid, _: macvpn_core::mfa::AuthResponseKind, _: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        Err(texts::not_yet())
    }
}
```

and `NativeController` (`list` → `Ok(vec![])`, `remote_address` → `None`, `start`/`stop` → `Err(texts::not_yet())`), `NativeProfiles` (`build` → `Ok(NativeProfile { data: Zeroizing::new(Vec::new()), file_name: String::new() })`, `install` → `Err(texts::not_yet())`).

```rust
impl SystemInfo for LinPlatform {
    fn home_directory(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        system::home()

    /// `$XDG_DATA_HOME` (default `<home>/.local/share`); the sandbox: `<home>/.local/share`.

    }

        system::local_time(unix).map(|(y, mo, d, ..)| dates::date((y, mo, d))).unwrap_or_default()
    }
    fn local_date_time(&self, unix: f64, language: AppLanguage) -> String {
        system::local_time(unix).map(|t| dates::date_time(t, language)).unwrap_or_default()

            .unwrap_or_default()

    fn app_location(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
```

## Task 13: the vault key in the Secret Service

**Goal:** `LinPlatform::key_store()` (outside the sandbox) keeps the vault's auto-unlock key (DEK) in the user's Secret Service (gnome-keyring), as macOS keeps it in the Keychain: plain `zbus` (see "Resolved points"), the `plain` session, the system's own unlock prompt for a locked collection, every copy zeroized.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/keystore.rs`, `crates/platform-linux/tests/keystore.rs`
- Modify: `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/{mod.rs,platform.rs}` (the `key_store` accessor only), `crates/platform-linux/src/lib.rs` (a test-only constructor export), `scripts/ignored-everywhere.txt`

- [ ] **Step 1: The failing tests, against a fake Secret Service on a private bus** — `crates/platform-linux/tests/keystore.rs`:

```rust
//! The Secret Service key store against an in-process fake of the few Secret Service calls it makes, served on the
//! private session bus of `dbus-run-session` (CI and `lin-remote.sh test` run every test under one). One test runs
//! against the real keyring at the desktop (`desktop:`, ignored everywhere).
#![cfg(target_os = "linux")]

use macvpn_core::vault::{KeyStore, KeyStoreError};
use macvpn_platform_linux::SecretServiceKeyStore;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, OnceLock};
use zbus::blocking::Connection;
use zbus::zvariant::{ObjectPath, OwnedObjectPath, OwnedValue, Value};

type Secret = (OwnedObjectPath, Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, String);

#[derive(Default)]
struct Store {
    /// item path → (attributes, secret)
    items: HashMap<String, (HashMap<String, String>, Vec<u8>)>,
    locked: bool,
    next: u32,
}

struct Service(Arc<Mutex<Store>>);
struct Collection(Arc<Mutex<Store>>);
struct Item(Arc<Mutex<Store>>, String);
struct Session;

#[zbus::interface(name = "org.freedesktop.Secret.Service")]
impl Service {
    fn open_session(&self, algorithm: String, _input: OwnedValue) -> zbus::fdo::Result<(OwnedValue, OwnedObjectPath)> {
        assert_eq!(algorithm, "plain");
        Ok((OwnedValue::try_from(Value::from("")).unwrap(), OwnedObjectPath::try_from("/org/freedesktop/secrets/session/s1").unwrap()))
    }
    fn search_items(&self, attributes: HashMap<String, String>) -> (Vec<OwnedObjectPath>, Vec<OwnedObjectPath>) {
        let store = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        let found: Vec<OwnedObjectPath> = store
            .items
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, (attrs, _))| attributes.iter().all(|(k, v)| attrs.get(k) == Some(v)))
            .map(|(p, _)| OwnedObjectPath::try_from(p.as_str()).unwrap())
            .collect();
        if store.locked { (vec![], found) } else { (found, vec![]) }
    }
    fn unlock(&self, objects: Vec<OwnedObjectPath>) -> (Vec<OwnedObjectPath>, OwnedObjectPath) {
        self.0.lock().unwrap().locked = false;
        (objects, OwnedObjectPath::try_from("/").unwrap())
    }
    fn read_alias(&self, name: String) -> OwnedObjectPath {
        assert_eq!(name, "default");
        OwnedObjectPath::try_from("/org/freedesktop/secrets/collection/login").unwrap()
    }
}

#[zbus::interface(name = "org.freedesktop.Secret.Collection")]
impl Collection {
    fn create_item(
        &self,
        properties: HashMap<String, OwnedValue>,
        secret: Secret,
        replace: bool,
        #[zbus(object_server)] server: &zbus::ObjectServer,
    ) -> zbus::fdo::Result<(OwnedObjectPath, OwnedObjectPath)> {
        assert!(replace);
        let attrs: HashMap<String, String> =
            HashMap::try_from(properties["org.freedesktop.Secret.Item.Attributes"].clone()).unwrap();
        let mut store = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        store.items.retain(|_, (a, _)| a != &attrs);
        store.next += 1;
        let path = format!("/org/freedesktop/secrets/collection/login/i{}", store.next);
        store.items.insert(path.clone(), (attrs, secret.2));
        drop(store);
        futures_lite_block(server.at(path.as_str(), Item(self.0.clone(), path.clone())));
        Ok((OwnedObjectPath::try_from(path).unwrap(), OwnedObjectPath::try_from("/").unwrap()))
    }
}

#[zbus::interface(name = "org.freedesktop.Secret.Item")]
impl Item {
    fn get_secret(&self, session: OwnedObjectPath) -> zbus::fdo::Result<Secret> {
        let store = self.0.lock().unwrap();
        let (_, value) = store.items.get(&self.1).ok_or_else(|| zbus::fdo::Error::UnknownObject(self.1.clone()))?;
        Ok((session, vec![], value.clone(), "application/octet-stream".into()))
    }
    fn delete(&self) -> OwnedObjectPath {
        self.0.lock().unwrap().items.remove(&self.1);
        OwnedObjectPath::try_from("/").unwrap()
    }
}

#[zbus::interface(name = "org.freedesktop.Secret.Session")]
impl Session {
    fn close(&self) {}
}

/// `ObjectServer::at` is async in the server-side API; block on it (zbus' own executor).
fn futures_lite_block<F: std::future::Future>(f: F) -> F::Output {
    zbus::block_on(f)
}

fn fake() -> Arc<Mutex<Store>> {
    static FAKE: OnceLock<(Connection, Arc<Mutex<Store>>)> = OnceLock::new();
    FAKE.get_or_init(|| {
        let store = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Store::default()));
        let conn = zbus::blocking::connection::Builder::session()
            .unwrap()
            .name("org.freedesktop.secrets")
            .unwrap()
            .serve_at("/org/freedesktop/secrets", Service(store.clone()))
            .unwrap()
            .serve_at("/org/freedesktop/secrets/collection/login", Collection(store.clone()))
            .unwrap()
            .serve_at("/org/freedesktop/secrets/session/s1", Session)
            .unwrap()
            .build()
            .expect("a session bus: run the tests under dbus-run-session");
        (conn, store)
    })
    .1
    .clone()
}

fn store_for_test() -> SecretServiceKeyStore {
    fake();
    SecretServiceKeyStore::for_tests(&format!("local.linvpn.test.{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()))
}

#[test]
fn set_get_exists_delete() {
    let ks = store_for_test();
    assert_eq!(ks.exists().unwrap(), false);
    assert!(ks.get().unwrap().is_none());
    ks.set(&[7u8; 32]).unwrap();
    assert!(ks.exists().unwrap());
    assert_eq!(ks.get().unwrap().unwrap().as_slice(), &[7u8; 32]);
    ks.set(&[9u8; 32]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(ks.get().unwrap().unwrap().as_slice(), &[9u8; 32], "replaced, not a second item");
    ks.delete().unwrap();
    assert!(!ks.exists().unwrap());
    ks.delete().unwrap(); // deleting nothing is fine
}

#[test]
fn a_locked_collection_is_unlocked_first() {
    let ks = store_for_test();
    ks.set(&[3u8; 32]).unwrap();
    fake().lock().unwrap().locked = true;
    assert_eq!(ks.get().unwrap().unwrap().as_slice(), &[3u8; 32]);
    ks.delete().unwrap();
}

#[test]
fn the_attributes_are_ours_only() {
    let ks = store_for_test();
    ks.set(&[1u8; 32]).unwrap();
    let store = fake();
    let store = store.lock().unwrap();
    let ours: Vec<_> = store.items.values().filter(|(a, _)| a.get("kind").map(String::as_str) == Some("vault-key")).collect();
    assert!(ours.iter().all(|(a, _)| a.len() == 2 && a["application"].starts_with("local.linvpn")));
}

#[test]
fn no_service_is_unavailable() {
    // A bus name nobody owns (and nothing can activate): the error is `Unavailable`, never a panic.
    let ks = SecretServiceKeyStore::for_tests_at("org.example.NoSuchSecrets", "local.linvpn.test.none");
    assert!(matches!(ks.get(), Err(KeyStoreError::Unavailable(_))));
}

/// At the desktop (CP-desktop login): the real gnome-keyring, a unique test item, deleted again.
#[test]
#[ignore = "desktop: the real Secret Service (gnome-keyring)"]
fn desktop_the_real_keyring_round_trip() {
    let ks = SecretServiceKeyStore::for_real_tests(&format!("local.linvpn.test.{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()));
    ks.set(&[5u8; 32]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(ks.get().unwrap().unwrap().as_slice(), &[5u8; 32]);
    ks.delete().unwrap();
    assert!(!ks.exists().unwrap());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `lin/keystore.rs`**

```rust
//! The vault's auto-unlock key (DEK) in the Secret Service (`org.freedesktop.secrets`: gnome-keyring), the
//! counterpart of the macOS Keychain item and the Windows credential. One item in the default collection, label
//! "LinVPN vault key", attributes `application = local.linvpn`, `kind = vault-key` (storage identifiers: literals).
//!
//! Protocol (Secret Service API 0.2): `OpenSession("plain")`; `SearchItems(attributes)` → unlocked / locked paths;
//! a locked item is unlocked with `Unlock` and, when a prompt is returned, the system's own prompt
//! (`Prompt.Prompt("")`, then the `Completed` signal, at most 120 s; dismissed → `Unavailable`); `GetSecret`;
//! `CreateItem(properties, secret, replace = true)` in the collection the alias `default` names; `Item.Delete`.
//! The `plain` session sends the DEK unencrypted over the session bus, which only this user's processes reach (the
//! same-user boundary every platform's key store has; LinVPN plan, "Resolved points"). Every copy is `Zeroizing`.
//! No session bus or no service: `Unavailable` (the vault then asks for the master password).

use macvpn_core::vault::{KeyStore, KeyStoreError};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::mpsc;
use std::time::Duration;
use zbus::blocking::{Connection, Proxy};
use zbus::zvariant::{ObjectPath, OwnedObjectPath, OwnedValue, Value};
use zeroize::Zeroizing;

const PATH: &str = "/org/freedesktop/secrets";
const LABEL: &str = "LinVPN vault key";
const APPLICATION: &str = "local.linvpn";
const PROMPT_WAIT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(120);

type Secret = (OwnedObjectPath, Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, String);

pub struct SecretServiceKeyStore {
    service: String,
    application: String,
    bus: fn() -> zbus::Result<Connection>,
}

fn unavailable(e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> KeyStoreError {
    KeyStoreError::Unavailable(format!("Secret Service: {e}"))
}

impl SecretServiceKeyStore {
    pub fn production() -> SecretServiceKeyStore {
        SecretServiceKeyStore { service: "org.freedesktop.secrets".into(), application: APPLICATION.into(), bus: crate::lin::bus::session }
    }

    /// Tests: a unique application attribute, on the test's session bus (a fake service).
    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn for_tests(application: &str) -> SecretServiceKeyStore {
        SecretServiceKeyStore { service: "org.freedesktop.secrets".into(), application: application.into(), bus: Connection::session }
    }

    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn for_tests_at(service: &str, application: &str) -> SecretServiceKeyStore {
        SecretServiceKeyStore { service: service.into(), application: application.into(), bus: Connection::session }
    }

    /// Desktop tests: the real keyring of the logged-in user, a unique application attribute.
    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn for_real_tests(application: &str) -> SecretServiceKeyStore {
        Self::for_tests(application)
    }

    fn attributes(&self) -> HashMap<&str, &str> {
        HashMap::from([("application", self.application.as_str()), ("kind", "vault-key")])
    }

    fn proxy<'a>(&self, bus: &'a Connection, path: &'a str, interface: &'a str) -> Result<Proxy<'a>, KeyStoreError> {
        Proxy::new(bus, self.service.clone(), path, interface).map_err(unavailable)
    }

    fn connect(&self) -> Result<(Connection, OwnedObjectPath), KeyStoreError> {
        let bus = (self.bus)().map_err(unavailable)?;
        let service = self.proxy(&bus, PATH, "org.freedesktop.Secret.Service")?;
        let (_, session): (OwnedValue, OwnedObjectPath) =
            service.call("OpenSession", &("plain", Value::from(""))).map_err(unavailable)?;
        Ok((bus, session))
    }

    fn close(&self, bus: &Connection, session: &OwnedObjectPath) {
        if let Ok(p) = Proxy::new(bus, self.service.clone(), session.as_str(), "org.freedesktop.Secret.Session") {
            let _: Result<(), _> = p.call("Close", &());
        }
    }

    /// The item's path, unlocked first if it is locked; `None`: no item.
    fn find(&self, bus: &Connection) -> Result<Option<OwnedObjectPath>, KeyStoreError> {
        let service = self.proxy(bus, PATH, "org.freedesktop.Secret.Service")?;
        let (unlocked, locked): (Vec<OwnedObjectPath>, Vec<OwnedObjectPath>) =
            service.call("SearchItems", &(self.attributes(),)).map_err(unavailable)?;
        if let Some(path) = unlocked.into_iter().next() {
            return Ok(Some(path));
        }
        let Some(path) = locked.into_iter().next() else { return Ok(None) };
        let (done, prompt): (Vec<OwnedObjectPath>, OwnedObjectPath) =
            service.call("Unlock", &(vec![path.clone()],)).map_err(unavailable)?;
        if done.contains(&path) {
            return Ok(Some(path));
        }
        self.run_prompt(bus, &prompt)?;
        Ok(Some(path))
    }

    /// The system's prompt: `Prompt("")`, then `Completed(dismissed, result)`.
    fn run_prompt(&self, bus: &Connection, prompt: &OwnedObjectPath) -> Result<(), KeyStoreError> {
        if prompt.as_str() == "/" {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let p = self.proxy(bus, prompt.as_str(), "org.freedesktop.Secret.Prompt")?;
        let signals = p.receive_signal("Completed").map_err(unavailable)?;
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            for message in signals {
                let dismissed = message.body().deserialize::<(bool, OwnedValue)>().map(|(d, _)| d).unwrap_or(true);
                let _ = tx.send(dismissed);
                break;
            }
        });
        p.call::<_, _, ()>("Prompt", &("",)).map_err(unavailable)?;
        match rx.recv_timeout(PROMPT_WAIT) {
            Ok(false) => Ok(()),
            Ok(true) => Err(KeyStoreError::Unavailable("the keyring was not unlocked".into())),
            Err(_) => {
                let _: Result<(), _> = p.call("Dismiss", &());
                Err(KeyStoreError::Unavailable("the keyring prompt timed out".into()))
            }
        }
    }
}

impl KeyStore for SecretServiceKeyStore {
    fn get(&self) -> Result<Option<Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>>, KeyStoreError> {
        let (bus, session) = self.connect()?;
        let result = (|| {
            let Some(path) = self.find(&bus)? else { return Ok(None) };
            let item = self.proxy(&bus, path.as_str(), "org.freedesktop.Secret.Item")?;
            let secret: Secret = item.call("GetSecret", &(&session,)).map_err(unavailable)?;
            let (_, mut parameters, value, _) = secret;
            parameters.clear();
            Ok(Some(Zeroizing::new(value)))
        })();
        self.close(&bus, &session);
        result
    }

    fn exists(&self) -> Result<bool, KeyStoreError> {
        let bus = (self.bus)().map_err(unavailable)?;
        let service = self.proxy(&bus, PATH, "org.freedesktop.Secret.Service")?;
        let (unlocked, locked): (Vec<OwnedObjectPath>, Vec<OwnedObjectPath>) =
            service.call("SearchItems", &(self.attributes(),)).map_err(unavailable)?;
        Ok(!unlocked.is_empty() || !locked.is_empty())
    }

    fn set(&self, key: &[u8]) -> Result<(), KeyStoreError> {
        let (bus, session) = self.connect()?;
        let result = (|| {
            let service = self.proxy(&bus, PATH, "org.freedesktop.Secret.Service")?;
            let collection: OwnedObjectPath = service.call("ReadAlias", &("default",)).map_err(unavailable)?;
            if collection.as_str() == "/" {
                return Err(KeyStoreError::Unavailable("no default keyring".into()));
            }
            let c = self.proxy(&bus, collection.as_str(), "org.freedesktop.Secret.Collection")?;
            let properties: HashMap<&str, Value> = HashMap::from([
                ("org.freedesktop.Secret.Item.Label", Value::from(LABEL)),
                ("org.freedesktop.Secret.Item.Attributes", Value::from(self.attributes())),
            ]);
            let value = Zeroizing::new(key.to_vec());
            let secret = (&session, Vec::<u8>::new(), value.as_slice(), "application/octet-stream");
            let (_, prompt): (OwnedObjectPath, OwnedObjectPath) =
                c.call("CreateItem", &(properties, secret, true)).map_err(unavailable)?;
            self.run_prompt(&bus, &prompt)
        })();
        self.close(&bus, &session);
        result
    }

    fn delete(&self) -> Result<(), KeyStoreError> {
        let bus = (self.bus)().map_err(unavailable)?;
        let Some(path) = self.find(&bus)? else { return Ok(()) };
        let item = self.proxy(&bus, path.as_str(), "org.freedesktop.Secret.Item")?;
        let prompt: OwnedObjectPath = item.call("Delete", &()).map_err(unavailable)?;
        self.run_prompt(&bus, &prompt)
    }
}
```

Notes for the implementer: (1) `ObjectPath` import is used by the `secret` tuple's session if the compiler wants a borrowed path; remove unused imports clippy flags. (2) `Value::from(self.attributes())` must serialize as `a{ss}`; if the pinned zbus needs `zvariant::Dict`, build it that way. (3) `get` must not hold the D-Bus reply buffer longer than the `Zeroizing` copy: deserialize straight into the tuple as above. (4) Never log the value.

`lin/mod.rs`: `pub mod keystore;`. `lib.rs`: `#[cfg(target_os = "linux")] pub use lin::keystore::SecretServiceKeyStore;`. `platform.rs`, the `key_store` accessor: `None => Arc::new(SecretServiceKeyStore::production())`.

## Task 14: the control API on Linux

**Goal:** paired apps reach LinVPN over `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock` with the same protocol and the same peer checks as macOS (uid, pid, executable); a runtime dir that is not ours or not 0700 keeps the control API off with the error shown; the example client finds the socket on Linux.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/control.rs`, `crates/platform-linux/tests/control.rs`
- Modify: `crates/platform-linux/src/lin/{mod.rs,platform.rs}` (the `control_transport` accessor), `crates/platform-linux/src/lib.rs`, `examples/macvpn-control-client/src/lib.rs`, `examples/macvpn-control-client/README.md` (one line)

```rust
use macvpn_platform_api::{Accepted, ControlTransport, PeerUser};
use macvpn_platform_linux::LinTransport;
use std::os::unix::fs::{PermissionsExt, symlink};
use std::os::unix::net::UnixStream;
use std::time::Duration;

fn temp_root(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("linvpn-control-{name}-{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()));
    std::fs::create_dir(&dir).unwrap();
    std::fs::set_permissions(&dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
    dir
}

#[test]
fn binds_in_the_runtime_dir_and_identifies_the_peer() {
    let root = temp_root("ok");
    let audit = temp_root("audit");
    let t = LinTransport::under(&root);
    t.prepare_dir(&audit.join("control")).unwrap();
    let bound = t.bind(&audit.join("control")).unwrap();
    let socket = root.join("linvpn/control/linvpn.sock");
    assert_eq!(bound.socket_file.as_deref(), Some(socket.as_path()));
    assert_eq!(bound.display, socket.display().to_string());
    for dir in [root.join("linvpn"), root.join("linvpn/control")] {
        assert_eq!(std::fs::metadata(&dir).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777, 0o700, "{}", dir.display());
    }
    let _client = UnixStream::connect(&socket).unwrap();
    let Accepted::Client { peer, .. } = bound.listener.accept(Duration::from_millis(500)).unwrap() else {
        panic!("no client")
    };
    let peer = peer.unwrap();
    assert!(peer.same_user);
    assert!(matches!(peer.user, PeerUser::Uid(_)));
    assert_eq!(peer.pid, std::process::id() as i32);
    assert_eq!(peer.executable.as_deref(), std::env::current_exe().unwrap().to_str());
    // A second bind while the first holds the lock: AddrInUse.
    assert_eq!(t.bind(&audit.join("control")).err().map(|e| e.kind()), Some(std::io::ErrorKind::AddrInUse));
}

    let root = temp_root("open");

    let e = LinTransport::under(&root).bind(&temp_root("a2")).err().unwrap();
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `lin/control.rs`**

```rust
//! The control API's transport on Linux: the shared Unix socket (`macvpn_unixsys`) at
//! `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock` (spec 3.1), with `linvpn.lock` beside it as the single-instance lock
//! of the control server. `bind(dir)` ignores `dir` for the socket (as Windows' pipe does); `prepare_dir(dir)` still
//! prepares the audit file's directory under the data dir. The runtime dir must be a real directory of this uid with
//! no group or other access (the XDG spec's 0700), else `PermissionDenied`: fail closed. The sandbox's runtime dir
//! (`<sandbox>/run`) is created 0700 by `prepare_dir` itself.

use macvpn_platform_api::{BoundEndpoint, ControlTransport};
use macvpn_unixsys::{control_socket, transport};
use std::io::{self, ErrorKind};
use std::os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, PermissionsExt};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

pub struct LinTransport {
    /// `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` (or the sandbox's `run`); `None`: unset.
    root: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// The sandbox creates its root; the real `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` must exist (logind makes it).
    create_root: bool,
}

impl LinTransport {
    pub fn production(runtime_dir: Option<PathBuf>) -> LinTransport {
        LinTransport { root: runtime_dir, create_root: false }
    }

    pub fn sandbox(run: PathBuf) -> LinTransport {
        LinTransport { root: Some(run), create_root: true }
    }

    /// Tests: an existing root.
    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn under(root: &Path) -> LinTransport {
        LinTransport { root: Some(root.to_path_buf()), create_root: false }
    }

    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn without_runtime_dir() -> LinTransport {
        LinTransport { root: None, create_root: false }
    }

    fn checked_root(&self) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
        let root = self.root.clone().ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(ErrorKind::Unsupported, "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is not set"))?;
        if self.create_root {
            control_socket::prepare_dir(&root)?;
        }
        let meta = std::fs::symlink_metadata(&root)?;
        // SAFETY: `getuid` takes no argument and cannot fail.
        let uid = unsafe { libc::getuid() };
        if !meta.is_dir() || meta.uid() != uid || meta.permissions().mode() & 0o077 != 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", root.display()),
            ));
        }
        Ok(root)
    }
}

impl ControlTransport for LinTransport {
    fn bind(&self, _dir: &Path) -> io::Result<BoundEndpoint> {
        let root = self.checked_root()?;
        let ours = root.join("linvpn");
        control_socket::prepare_dir(&ours)?;
        let control = ours.join("control");
        control_socket::prepare_dir(&control)?;
        let bound = control_socket::bind_at(&control, "linvpn.sock", "linvpn.lock")?;
        transport::endpoint(bound, control.join("linvpn.sock"))
    }

    fn random_32(&self) -> io::Result<[u8; 32]> {
        macvpn_unixsys::random::random_32()
    }

    fn is_already_closed(&self, e: &io::Error) -> bool {
        control_socket::is_already_closed(e)
    }

    fn prepare_dir(&self, dir: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
        control_socket::prepare_dir(dir)
    }
}
```

`lin/mod.rs`: `pub mod control;`. `lib.rs`: `#[cfg(target_os = "linux")] pub use lin::control::LinTransport;`. `platform.rs`, the `control_transport` accessor:

```rust
    /// `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock`; the sandbox: `<sandbox>/run/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock`
    /// (`MACVPN_SANDBOX_HOME`, read here because the transport is created before the environment).
    fn control_transport(&self) -> Arc<dyn ControlTransport> {
        if self.is_sandbox() {
            #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
            if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("MACVPN_SANDBOX_HOME").map(PathBuf::from).filter(|h| h.is_absolute()) {
                return Arc::new(LinTransport::sandbox(home.join("run")));
            }
            return Arc::new(LinTransport::without_runtime_dir());
        }
        let home = system::home().unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/nonexistent"));
        Arc::new(LinTransport::production(Xdg::from_env(&home, |k| std::env::var_os(k)).runtime_dir))
    }
```

(The sandbox variable's name must not appear in a release binary: the `#[cfg(debug_assertions)]` keeps it out, as in platform-win's `ras()`; `build-deb.sh` gates it, Task 21.)

```rust
/// `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock`, where LinVPN listens while "Allow other apps to control VPNs" is
/// on.
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub fn default_socket_path() -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
    let run = std::env::var_os("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .filter(|p| p.is_absolute())
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(ErrorKind::NotFound, "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is not set"))?;
    Ok(run.join("linvpn/control/linvpn.sock"))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The pure code** — `pure/portal.rs`:

```rust
//! The desktop portal's values: `org.freedesktop.appearance` `color-scheme` (0 no preference, 1 dark, 2 light) and
//! the FileChooser's `uris` (only `file:///…`, percent-decoded; anything else is dropped).

use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStringExt;
use std::path::PathBuf;

pub fn dark_from_color_scheme(value: u32) -> Option<bool> {
    match value {
        1 => Some(true),
        2 => Some(false),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn percent_decode(s: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(b.len());
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < b.len() {
        if b[i] == b'%' {
            let hex = std::str::from_utf8(b.get(i + 1..i + 3)?).ok()?;
            let byte = u8::from_str_radix(hex, 16).ok()?;
            if byte == 0 {
                return None;
            }
            out.push(byte);
            i += 3;
        } else {
            out.push(b[i]);
            i += 1;
        }
    }
    Some(out)
}

pub fn paths_from_uris(uris: &[String]) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    uris.iter()
        .filter_map(|u| u.strip_prefix("file://"))
        .filter(|rest| rest.starts_with('/'))
        .filter_map(percent_decode)
        .map(|bytes| PathBuf::from(OsString::from_vec(bytes)))
        .collect()
}
```

(`OsStringExt` is Unix-only; `pure` must compile on macOS too: it does (macOS is Unix). Windows never builds platform-linux's pure tests? It does, in the windows CI job only if the crate is in its package list: it is not (`-p` list). Keep it so; note it in the module doc.)

`pure/mark.rs`:

```rust
//! The download mark on Linux: the `user.xdg.origin.url` extended attribute browsers set on downloads. Fail closed:
//! only "absent", "the file system has no user xattrs" and "no such file" are unmarked.

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum XattrRead {
    Present,
    /// `ENODATA`.
    Absent,
    /// `ENOTSUP`: no mark can exist there.
    NotSupported,
    /// `ENOENT`.
    NoFile,
    /// Any other errno.
    Error(i32),
}

pub fn is_marked(read: XattrRead) -> bool {
    matches!(read, XattrRead::Present | XattrRead::Error(_))
}
```

`pure/notify.rs`:

```rust
//! Which notification id shows which identifier, and its user info (in memory: a click after a restart opens
//! nothing, as on Windows).

use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap};

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Book {
    by_identifier: HashMap<String, u32>,
    info: HashMap<u32, (String, BTreeMap<String, String>)>,
}

impl Book {
    /// The id a new post of `identifier` replaces (0: none).
    pub fn replaces(&self, identifier: &str) -> u32 {
        self.by_identifier.get(identifier).copied().unwrap_or(0)
    }

    pub fn posted(&mut self, identifier: &str, id: u32, user_info: (&str, &str)) {
        if let Some(old) = self.by_identifier.insert(identifier.to_string(), id) {
            self.info.remove(&old);
        }
        self.info.insert(id, (identifier.to_string(), BTreeMap::from([(user_info.0.to_string(), user_info.1.to_string())])));
    }

    pub fn clicked(&self, id: u32) -> Option<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
        self.info.get(&id).map(|(_, i)| i.clone())
    }

    pub fn id_of(&self, identifier: &str) -> Option<u32> {
        self.by_identifier.get(identifier).copied()
    }

    pub fn closed(&mut self, id: u32) {
        if let Some((identifier, _)) = self.info.remove(&id)
            && self.by_identifier.get(&identifier) == Some(&id)
        {
            self.by_identifier.remove(&identifier);
        }
    }
}
```

`lin/notify.rs`:

```rust
//! Notifications through `org.freedesktop.Notifications` (GNOME Shell's server): app name "LinVPN", the hint
//! `desktop-entry = linvpn` (GNOME shows the app's icon and groups them), the default action (a click), and
//! `replaces_id` so a repost of one identifier replaces the shown notification. `ActionInvoked(id, "default")` calls
//! the click handler with that notification's user info; `NotificationClosed` forgets it.

use crate::pure::notify::Book;
use macvpn_platform_api::Notifier;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, PoisonError};
use zbus::blocking::{Connection, Proxy};
use zbus::zvariant::Value;

const DEST: &str = "org.freedesktop.Notifications";
const PATH: &str = "/org/freedesktop/Notifications";

type OnClick = Box<dyn Fn(BTreeMap<String, String>) + Send + Sync>;

pub struct LinNotifier {
    bus: fn() -> zbus::Result<Connection>,
    book: Arc<Mutex<Book>>,
}

impl LinNotifier {
    pub fn production() -> LinNotifier {
        LinNotifier { bus: crate::lin::bus::session, book: Arc::default() }
    }

    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn for_tests() -> LinNotifier {
        LinNotifier { bus: Connection::session, book: Arc::default() }
    }

    fn proxy(&self) -> Option<(Connection, Proxy<'static>)> {
        let bus = (self.bus)().ok()?;
        let proxy = Proxy::new(&bus, DEST, PATH, DEST).ok()?;
        Some((bus, proxy))
    }
}

impl Notifier for LinNotifier {
    fn start(&self, on_click: OnClick) {
        let Some((_bus, proxy)) = self.proxy() else { return };
        let book = self.book.clone();
        let Ok(invoked) = proxy.receive_signal("ActionInvoked") else { return };
        let Ok(closed) = proxy.receive_signal("NotificationClosed") else { return };
        let closed_book = book.clone();
        std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("linvpn-notify-closed".into())
            .spawn(move || {
                for m in closed {
                    if let Ok((id, _reason)) = m.body().deserialize::<(u32, u32)>() {
                        closed_book.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner).closed(id);
                    }
                }
            })
            .ok();
        std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("linvpn-notify-click".into())
            .spawn(move || {
                for m in invoked {
                    let Ok((id, action)) = m.body().deserialize::<(u32, String)>() else { continue };
                    if action != "default" {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let info = book.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner).clicked(id);
                    if let Some(info) = info {
                        on_click(info);
                    }
                }
            })
            .ok();
    }

    fn post(&self, identifier: &str, title: &str, body: &str, user_info: (&str, &str)) {
        let Some((_bus, proxy)) = self.proxy() else { return };
        let replaces = self.book.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner).replaces(identifier);
        let hints: HashMap<&str, Value> = HashMap::from([("desktop-entry", Value::from("linvpn"))]);
        let reply: zbus::Result<u32> =
            proxy.call("Notify", &("LinVPN", replaces, "linvpn", title, body, vec!["default", ""], hints, -1i32));
        if let Ok(id) = reply {
            self.book.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner).posted(identifier, id, user_info);
        }
    }

    fn remove_delivered(&self, identifier: &str) {
        let id = self.book.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner).id_of(identifier);
        if let (Some(id), Some((_bus, proxy))) = (id, self.proxy()) {
            let _: zbus::Result<()> = proxy.call("CloseNotification", &(id,));
        }
    }
}
```

(`Proxy<'static>` from a borrowed connection: zbus' blocking `Proxy::new` takes `&Connection` and clones it inside; if the pinned version ties the lifetime, return the `Connection` and build the proxy at each call site.) Accessor: `fn notifier(&self) -> &dyn Notifier` returns a `LinNotifier::production()` field of `LinPlatform` (the sandbox: the inert notifier; the smoke run records notices itself).

Run (VM): `LINVPN_REMOTE_DIR=linvpn-work-slot1 scripts/lin-remote.sh run 'dbus-run-session -- cargo test --locked -p macvpn-platform-linux --test notify_bus'` → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 5: The portal: color scheme, its changes, and the file chooser** — `lin/portal.rs`:

```rust
//! `org.freedesktop.portal.Desktop` on the session bus: `Settings.ReadOne("org.freedesktop.appearance",
//! "color-scheme")` (older portals: `Read`, which wraps the value once more), its `SettingChanged` signal, and the
//! `FileChooser`'s `OpenFile` / `SaveFile` with the `Response` signal on the returned request object.

use crate::pure::portal::{dark_from_color_scheme, paths_from_uris};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use zbus::blocking::Proxy;
use zbus::zvariant::{OwnedObjectPath, OwnedValue, Value};

const DEST: &str = "org.freedesktop.portal.Desktop";
const PATH: &str = "/org/freedesktop/portal/desktop";

fn unwrap_u32(v: &OwnedValue) -> Option<u32> {
    if let Ok(n) = u32::try_from(v) {
        return Some(n);
    }
    // `Read` returns `v` inside `v`.
    let inner: OwnedValue = v.downcast_ref::<Value>().ok()?.try_into().ok()?;
    u32::try_from(&inner).ok()
}

/// `Some(true)` dark, `Some(false)` light, `None` no preference or no portal.
pub fn system_dark() -> Option<bool> {
    let bus = crate::lin::bus::session().ok()?;
    let settings = Proxy::new(&bus, DEST, PATH, "org.freedesktop.portal.Settings").ok()?;
    let value: OwnedValue = settings
        .call("ReadOne", &("org.freedesktop.appearance", "color-scheme"))
        .or_else(|_| settings.call("Read", &("org.freedesktop.appearance", "color-scheme")))
        .ok()?;
    dark_from_color_scheme(unwrap_u32(&value)?)
}

/// Calls `on_change` on its own thread whenever `color-scheme` changes.
pub fn watch_color_scheme(on_change: Box<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>) {
    let Ok(bus) = crate::lin::bus::session() else { return };
    let Ok(settings) = Proxy::new(&bus, DEST, PATH, "org.freedesktop.portal.Settings") else { return };
    let Ok(changes) = settings.receive_signal("SettingChanged") else { return };
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("linvpn-color-scheme".into())
        .spawn(move || {
            for m in changes {
                if let Ok((ns, key, _)) = m.body().deserialize::<(String, String, OwnedValue)>()
                    && ns == "org.freedesktop.appearance"
                    && key == "color-scheme"
                {
                    on_change();
                }
            }
        })
        .ok();
}

/// A blocking file-chooser request; call it off the main thread. `save`: `SaveFile` with `suggested` as the
/// current name. The paths the user chose; empty on cancel or error.
pub fn choose(save: bool, multiple: bool, suggested: &str, title: &str) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    let Ok(bus) = crate::lin::bus::session() else { return Vec::new() };
    let Ok(chooser) = Proxy::new(&bus, DEST, PATH, "org.freedesktop.portal.FileChooser") else { return Vec::new() };
    let token = format!("linvpn{}", std::process::id());
    let mut options: HashMap<&str, Value> = HashMap::from([("handle_token", Value::from(token.as_str())), ("modal", Value::from(true))]);
    if save {
        options.insert("current_name", Value::from(suggested));
    } else {
        options.insert("multiple", Value::from(multiple));
    }
    let method = if save { "SaveFile" } else { "OpenFile" };
    // Subscribe before the call: the request path is predictable from the sender and the token
    // (/org/freedesktop/portal/desktop/request/<sender with . → _ and no :>/<token>).
    let sender = bus.unique_name().map(|n| n.trim_start_matches(':').replace('.', "_")).unwrap_or_default();
    let request_path = format!("/org/freedesktop/portal/desktop/request/{sender}/{token}");
    let Ok(request) = Proxy::new(&bus, DEST, request_path.as_str(), "org.freedesktop.portal.Request") else { return Vec::new() };
    let Ok(mut responses) = request.receive_signal("Response") else { return Vec::new() };
    let reply: zbus::Result<OwnedObjectPath> = chooser.call(method, &("", title, options));
    if reply.is_err() {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let Some(m) = responses.next() else { return Vec::new() };
    let Ok((code, results)) = m.body().deserialize::<(u32, HashMap<String, OwnedValue>)>() else { return Vec::new() };
    if code != 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let uris: Vec<String> = results.get("uris").and_then(|v| Vec::<String>::try_from(v.clone()).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
    paths_from_uris(&uris)
}
```

`FilePanels` for `LinPlatform` (the `done` callbacks are not `Send`: they wait in a main-thread queue; the worker thread hands the result back to the GTK main context, which runs on the main thread):

```rust
thread_local! {
    static PENDING: std::cell::RefCell<HashMap<u64, Box<dyn FnOnce(Vec<PathBuf>)>>> = Default::default();
}
static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(1);

fn ask(save: bool, multiple: bool, suggested: String, title: String, done: Box<dyn FnOnce(Vec<PathBuf>)>) {
    let id = NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    PENDING.with(|p| p.borrow_mut().insert(id, done));
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let paths = portal::choose(save, multiple, &suggested, &title);
        glib::MainContext::default().invoke(move || {
            if let Some(done) = PENDING.with(|p| p.borrow_mut().remove(&id)) {
                done(paths);
            }
        });
    });
}

impl FilePanels for Panels {
    fn choose_files(&self, _parent: NativeWindow, multiple: bool, done: Box<dyn FnOnce(Vec<PathBuf>)>) {
        ask(false, multiple, String::new(), l10n::tr("Open", &[]), done);
    }
    fn choose_save_path(&self, _parent: NativeWindow, suggested: &str, message: Option<&str>, done: Box<dyn FnOnce(Option<PathBuf>)>) {
        let title = message.map(str::to_string).unwrap_or_else(|| l10n::tr("Save", &[]));
        ask(true, false, suggested.to_string(), title, Box::new(move |paths| done(paths.into_iter().next())));
    }
}
```

("Open" and "Save" must be existing `strings.json` keys; if not, add them with `"only": ["linux"]` and Hungarian "Megnyitás" / "Mentés".) The `appearance` part of `WindowChrome`: `fn system_dark(&self) -> Option<bool> { portal::system_dark() }` (the sandbox: also the portal: it is read-only and harmless). `AppLifecycle::observe_appearance` is Task 16's (it calls `portal::watch_color_scheme`).

- [ ] **Step 6: Clipboard and window sizes over GTK** — `lin/clipboard.rs`:

```rust
//! The clipboard through GTK 3 (the app is a GTK app; Wayland gives the clipboard only to a focused client, which
//! GTK handles). Main thread only: every call is moved there with `glib::MainContext::default().invoke` and waited
//! for. A secret goes out with the extra target `x-kde-passwordManagerHint` = `secret`, which clipboard managers that
//! honour it (KDE's Klipper, GNOME extensions that implement it) leave out of their history. The mark is a counter;
//! the secret is kept (zeroized when replaced) only to clear the clipboard if it still holds it.

use macvpn_platform_api::{ClipboardMark, PlatformError};
use std::sync::mpsc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicIsize, Ordering};
use zeroize::Zeroizing;

static GENERATION: AtomicIsize = AtomicIsize::new(0);

thread_local! {
    static LAST: std::cell::RefCell<Option<(isize, Zeroizing<String>)>> = const { std::cell::RefCell::new(None) };
}

fn on_main<T: Send + 'static>(f: impl FnOnce() -> T + Send + 'static) -> Option<T> {
    let context = glib::MainContext::default();
    if context.is_owner() {
        return Some(f());
    }
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
    context.invoke(move || {
        let _ = tx.send(f());
    });
    rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
}

fn clipboard() -> gtk::Clipboard {
    gtk::Clipboard::get(&gdk::SELECTION_CLIPBOARD)
}

pub fn write_plain(text: &str) -> Result<(), PlatformError> {
    let text = text.to_string();
    on_main(move || clipboard().set_text(&text)).ok_or_else(|| PlatformError::Failed("the clipboard is busy".into()))
}

pub fn write_secret(text: &str) -> Result<ClipboardMark, PlatformError> {
    let secret = Zeroizing::new(text.to_string());
    on_main(move || {
        let targets = [
            gtk::TargetEntry::new("UTF8_STRING", gtk::TargetFlags::empty(), 0),
            gtk::TargetEntry::new("text/plain;charset=utf-8", gtk::TargetFlags::empty(), 0),
            gtk::TargetEntry::new("x-kde-passwordManagerHint", gtk::TargetFlags::empty(), 1),
        ];
        let for_get = secret.clone();
        let set = clipboard().set_with_data(&targets, move |_, data, info| {
            if info == 1 {
                data.set(&data.target(), 8, b"secret");
            } else {
                data.set_text(&for_get);
            }
        });
        if !set {
            return Err(PlatformError::Failed("the clipboard refused the text".into()));
        }
        let mark = GENERATION.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;
        LAST.with(|l| *l.borrow_mut() = Some((mark, secret)));
        Ok(ClipboardMark(mark))
    })
    .unwrap_or_else(|| Err(PlatformError::Failed("the clipboard is busy".into())))
}

pub fn clear_if_unchanged(mark: ClipboardMark) -> bool {
    on_main(move || {
        LAST.with(|l| {
            let mut last = l.borrow_mut();
            let Some((m, secret)) = last.as_ref() else { return false };
            if *m != mark.0 || clipboard().wait_for_text().as_deref() != Some(secret.as_str()) {
                return false;
            }
            clipboard().set_text("");
            clipboard().clear();
            *last = None;
            true
        })
    })
    .unwrap_or(false)
}
```

(`set_with_data`'s closure signature follows gtk-rs 0.18; adapt the parameter list to it. Add `gdk = "=0.18.2"` to the Linux dependencies: already in `Cargo.lock`.) `SecretClipboard for Clipboard { write_secret, clear_if_unchanged }` delegates; `Shell::copy_text` → `clipboard::write_plain`.

`lin/chrome.rs`:

```rust
//! Window sizes through GTK. `NativeWindow` is the `GtkWindow *` the app passes (`gtk_window().as_ptr()`, Task 18).
//! Square corners on Linux (LinVPN plan, "Resolved points"); the tray icon is drawn white (GNOME's top bar is dark
//! in both themes).

use macvpn_platform_api::{NativeWindow, WindowChrome};

pub struct Chrome;

fn window(w: NativeWindow) -> Option<gtk::Window> {
    if w.0.is_null() {
        return None;
    }
    use glib::translate::FromGlibPtrNone;
    // SAFETY: the app passes the pointer of a live GtkWindow on the main thread (the trait's contract); a new
    // reference is taken (`from_glib_none`) and dropped at the end of the call.
    Some(unsafe { gtk::Window::from_glib_none(w.0 as *mut gtk::ffi::GtkWindow) })
}

impl WindowChrome for Chrome {
    fn popover_corner_radius(&self) -> f64 {
        0.0
    }
    fn round_corners(&self, _w: NativeWindow, _radius: f64) -> bool {
        false
    }
    fn fit_viewport(&self, w: NativeWindow, width: f64, viewport: f64, center: bool) -> Option<f64> {
        let window = window(w)?;
        let height = viewport.ceil();
        window.resize(width.round() as i32, height as i32);
        if center {
            window.set_position(gtk::WindowPosition::Center);
        }
        Some(height)
    }
    fn order_front_regardless(&self, w: NativeWindow) {
        if let Some(window) = window(w) {
            window.present();
        }
    }
    fn tray_tint(&self) -> Option<[u8; 3]> {
        Some([0xff, 0xff, 0xff])
    }
    fn system_dark(&self) -> Option<bool> {
        crate::lin::portal::system_dark()
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The download mark** — `lin/xattr.rs`:

```rust
//! `getxattr(path, "user.xdg.origin.url")` (the size only; the value is never read).

use crate::pure::mark::XattrRead;
use std::ffi::CString;
use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
use std::path::Path;

pub fn origin(path: &Path) -> XattrRead {
    let Ok(c) = CString::new(path.as_os_str().as_bytes()) else { return XattrRead::Error(libc::EINVAL) };
    // SAFETY: two NUL-terminated strings that live for the call; a null buffer with size 0 asks for the size only.
    let n = unsafe { libc::getxattr(c.as_ptr(), c"user.xdg.origin.url".as_ptr(), std::ptr::null_mut(), 0) };
    if n >= 0 {
        return XattrRead::Present;
    }
    match std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() {
        Some(libc::ENODATA) => XattrRead::Absent,
        Some(libc::ENOTSUP) => XattrRead::NotSupported,
        Some(libc::ENOENT) => XattrRead::NoFile,
        Some(e) => XattrRead::Error(e),
        None => XattrRead::Error(0),
    }
}
```

A Linux test in `crates/platform-linux/tests/mark.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
fn a_downloaded_file_is_marked() {
    use macvpn_platform_api::{DownloadMark, Platform};
    let p = macvpn_platform_linux::LinPlatform::new(true);
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("linvpn-mark-{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()));
    std::fs::create_dir(&dir).unwrap();
    let f = dir.join("a.ovpn");
    std::fs::write(&f, "client\n").unwrap();
    assert!(!p.download_mark().is_marked(&f));
    let set = std::process::Command::new("setfattr").args(["-n", "user.xdg.origin.url", "-v", "https://example.com/a.ovpn"]).arg(&f).status();
    match set {
        Ok(s) if s.success() => assert!(p.download_mark().is_marked(&f)),
        _ => eprintln!("skipped the marked case: setfattr or user xattrs unavailable here"),
    }
    assert!(!p.download_mark().is_marked(&dir.join("missing.ovpn")));
}
```

(`setfattr` is in the `attr` package; without it the marked half is skipped and says so: the pure test covers the decision. Do not add an apt step for it.)

```rust
use macvpn_platform_linux::pure::instance::{bus_name, launch_files};

#[test]
fn the_installed_name_and_one_per_sandbox() {
    assert_eq!(bus_name(None), "local.linvpn.Instance");
    let a = bus_name(Some(Path::new("/tmp/sb-a")));
    let b = bus_name(Some(Path::new("/tmp/sb-b")));
```

```rust
use macvpn_platform_linux::pure::autostart::{State, desktop_file, state_of};

#[test]
fn our_file_is_on() {
    let text = desktop_file();
    assert!(text.starts_with("[Desktop Entry]\n"));
    assert!(text.contains("\nExec=/usr/bin/linvpn\n"));
    assert!(text.contains("\nX-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=true\n"));
    assert_eq!(state_of(Some(&text)), State::On);
}

#[test]
fn missing_or_turned_off_elsewhere() {
    assert_eq!(state_of(None), State::Off);
    let hidden = desktop_file().replace("X-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=true", "Hidden=true");
    assert_eq!(state_of(Some(&hidden)), State::TurnedOffElsewhere);
    let disabled = desktop_file().replace("X-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=true", "X-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=false");
    assert_eq!(state_of(Some(&disabled)), State::TurnedOffElsewhere);
    // Keys in other groups do not count.
    let other = format!("{}\n[Desktop Action x]\nHidden=true\n", desktop_file());
    assert_eq!(state_of(Some(&other)), State::On);
}
```
