---

## Task 22: CI and the release workflow

**Goal:** CI builds, lints and tests Linux on every push (an Ubuntu x64 job) and builds and gates both `.deb`s (amd64 and arm64 runners); the release workflow builds the `.deb`s on every tag and attaches them (with `.sha256` and provenance) only when `LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE` is `true`; the macOS job runs the new script tests.

**Files:**
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`, `scripts/test-sign-release.sh` (the provenance pattern check)

- [ ] **Step 1: The script tests in the macOS job** — in `ci.yml`'s `rust` job, after "Windows smoke script (print mode, no SSH)":

```yaml
      - name: Linux VM scripts (print mode, no SSH)
        run: |
          scripts/test-lin-remote.sh
          scripts/test-lin-smoke.sh
      - name: Linux package script (control file, syntax)
        run: scripts/test-build-deb.sh
```

(`test-lin-smoke.sh` arrives with Task 23; if Task 22 merges first, add that line in Task 23 instead.)

- [ ] **Step 2: The Linux jobs** — append to `ci.yml`'s jobs:

```yaml
  linux:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - name: Build dependencies (Tauri's Linux prerequisites; dbus for the private session bus of the tests)
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install --yes --no-install-recommends build-essential libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev \
            libayatana-appindicator3-dev librsvg2-dev libxdo-dev libssl-dev dbus
      - name: Rust toolchain (Linux x64)
        run: |
          RUST_VERSION=$(sed -n 's/^channel = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' rust-toolchain.toml)
          [[ "$RUST_VERSION" =~ ^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]] || { echo "rust-toolchain.toml: channel must be an exact version"; exit 1; }
          rustup toolchain install "$RUST_VERSION" --profile minimal -c clippy,rustfmt -t x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
          [ "$(rustc --version | cut -d' ' -f2)" = "$RUST_VERSION" ] || { echo "rustc is not $RUST_VERSION"; exit 1; }
          rustc --version
          cargo --version
      # Rust build cache: see job rust (checks only, never release artifacts; saved only on main and develop).
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@d1527c8f67ef9af357290475d4af4b0fab8f12e0 # v2.9.3
        with:
          shared-key: linux-x64-check
          save-if: ${{ github.event_name == 'push' && (github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' || github.ref == 'refs/heads/develop') }}
          cache-bin: "false"
      - name: cargo clippy
        run: cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings
      # Every test binary, under one private session bus (the D-Bus tests serve their own fakes on it; nothing touches
      # a desktop). The `desktop:` tests stay ignored (scripts/ignored-everywhere.txt; they run at the VM's desktop).
      - name: cargo test (the Linux test set, the app included)
        run: CI=1 dbus-run-session -- cargo test --locked -p macvpn-core -p macvpn-app-core -p macvpn-platform-api -p macvpn-platform-linux -p macvpn-unixsys -p macvpn-helper -p macvpn-control-client -p macvpn-app --lib --tests --no-fail-fast
      - name: Ignored tests are the same on every OS
        run: scripts/check-ignored.sh
  deb:
    strategy:
      matrix:
        include:
          - runner: ubuntu-24.04
            arch: amd64
            target: x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
          - runner: ubuntu-24.04-arm
            arch: arm64
            target: aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version: "26.8.2"
          # No automatic cache: release builds must not restore a cache written elsewhere.
          package-manager-cache: false
      - name: Build dependencies
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install --yes --no-install-recommends build-essential libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev \
            libayatana-appindicator3-dev librsvg2-dev libxdo-dev libssl-dev dpkg-dev desktop-file-utils
      - name: Rust toolchain
        env:
          TARGET: ${{ matrix.target }}
        run: |
          RUST_VERSION=$(sed -n 's/^channel = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' rust-toolchain.toml)
          [[ "$RUST_VERSION" =~ ^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]] || { echo "rust-toolchain.toml: channel must be an exact version"; exit 1; }
          rustup toolchain install "$RUST_VERSION" --profile minimal -t "$TARGET"
          [ "$(rustc --version | cut -d' ' -f2)" = "$RUST_VERSION" ] || { echo "rustc is not $RUST_VERSION"; exit 1; }
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@d1527c8f67ef9af357290475d4af4b0fab8f12e0 # v2.9.3
        with:
          shared-key: linux-${{ matrix.arch }}-release
          save-if: ${{ github.event_name == 'push' && (github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' || github.ref == 'refs/heads/develop') }}
          cache-bin: "false"
      - name: .deb with the release gates
        run: scripts/build-deb.sh
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        with:
          name: linvpn-deb-${{ matrix.arch }}
          path: |
            dist/linux/linvpn_*.deb
            dist/linux/linvpn_*.deb.sha256
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 7
```

Check locally: `ruby -ryaml -e 'YAML.load_file(".github/workflows/ci.yml")'` (or `python3 -c 'import yaml,sys; yaml.safe_load(open(".github/workflows/ci.yml"))'`) → no error.

- [ ] **Step 3: The release workflow** — in `release.yml`:

1. A job after `build-windows` (Hungarian comments, as the file's):

```yaml
  # 1c. LinVPN build Linuxon (amd64 és arm64, natív runnereken): csak olvasási jog, titok nélkül, cache nélkül. Minden
  #     tagnél lefut; a .deb-eket a release job csak akkor teszi a piszkozatba, ha a LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE repóváltozó
  #     értéke `true` (2.1.0-ig nincs beállítva).
  build-linux:
    needs: ci-green
    strategy:
      matrix:
        include:
          - runner: ubuntu-24.04
            arch: amd64
            target: x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
          - runner: ubuntu-24.04-arm
            arch: arm64
            target: aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - name: Tag ellenőrzése
        run: |
          [[ "$GITHUB_REF_NAME" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]] || { echo "Invalid tag: $GITHUB_REF_NAME"; exit 1; }
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
          fetch-depth: 0
      - name: A tagelt commit a main ágon van
        run: |
          git fetch --no-tags origin main
          git merge-base --is-ancestor "$GITHUB_SHA" origin/main || { echo "The tagged commit $GITHUB_SHA is not on main"; exit 1; }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version: "26.8.2"
          package-manager-cache: false
      - name: A verzió egyezik a taggel
        run: |
          V="${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v}"
          CONF=$(node -p "require('./app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json').version")
          [ "$CONF" = "$V" ] || { echo "tauri.conf.json version ($CONF) != tag ($V)"; exit 1; }
      - name: Build-függőségek
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install --yes --no-install-recommends build-essential libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev \
            libayatana-appindicator3-dev librsvg2-dev libxdo-dev libssl-dev dpkg-dev desktop-file-utils
      - name: Toolchain verzió
        env:
          TARGET: ${{ matrix.target }}
        run: |
          RUST_VERSION=$(sed -n 's/^channel = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' rust-toolchain.toml)
          [[ "$RUST_VERSION" =~ ^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]] || { echo "rust-toolchain.toml: channel must be an exact version"; exit 1; }
          rustup toolchain install "$RUST_VERSION" --profile minimal -t "$TARGET"
          [ "$(rustc --version | cut -d' ' -f2)" = "$RUST_VERSION" ] || { echo "rustc is not $RUST_VERSION"; exit 1; }
      # Tiszta build, cache nélkül, mint a többi build jobban.
      - name: .deb a kiadási kapukkal
        run: scripts/build-deb.sh
      - name: A kész .deb ellenőrzése
        env:
          ARCH: ${{ matrix.arch }}
        run: |
          V="${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v}"
          [ -f "dist/linux/linvpn_${V}_${ARCH}.deb" ] && [ -f "dist/linux/linvpn_${V}_${ARCH}.deb.sha256" ] || { echo "missing linvpn_${V}_${ARCH}.deb or its .sha256"; exit 1; }
          cat dist/linux/linvpn_*.deb.sha256
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        with:
          name: deb-${{ matrix.arch }}
          path: |
            dist/linux/linvpn_*.deb
            dist/linux/linvpn_*.deb.sha256
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 7
```

2. The `release` job: `needs: [build, build-windows, build-linux]`; its `if:` adds `&& (needs.build-linux.result == 'success' || vars.LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE != 'true')`; a download step

```yaml
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@9000827ccba6bdab643e8b6fd33ac0654aef8333 # v8.0.2
        if: vars.LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE == 'true'
        with:
          pattern: deb-*
          merge-multiple: true
          path: dist
```

the SHA-256 step adds `if [ "${LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE:-}" = true ]; then (cd dist && sha256sum -c linvpn_*.deb.sha256); fi` (with `LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE: ${{ vars.LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE }}` in its `env`); a provenance step

```yaml
      - name: Build provenance (deb)
        if: vars.LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE == 'true'
        uses: actions/attest-build-provenance@4d101475d8b20a2381f78447822ac1eab6504dd8 # v4.2.2
        with:
          subject-path: |
            dist/linvpn_*.deb
            dist/linvpn_*.deb.sha256
```

and in the "Release" step (env `LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE`), after the WinVPN block:

```bash
          if [ "${LINVPN_DEB_IN_RELEASE:-}" = true ]; then
            V="${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v}"
            # Mindkét .deb vagy egyik sem: a sign-release.sh csak így ír alá.
            for arch in amd64 arm64; do
              [ -f "dist/linvpn_${V}_${arch}.deb" ] && [ -f "dist/linvpn_${V}_${arch}.deb.sha256" ] || { echo "missing linvpn_${V}_${arch}.deb or its .sha256"; exit 1; }
            done
            (cd dist && sha256sum -c linvpn_*.deb.sha256)
            FILES+=(dist/linvpn_*.deb dist/linvpn_*.deb.sha256)
            TITLE="$TITLE + LinVPN $GITHUB_REF_NAME"
            NOTES+=" LinVPN: a linvpn_X.Y.Z_amd64.deb és a linvpn_X.Y.Z_arm64.deb Debian- és Ubuntu-alapú asztali gépekre (Ubuntu 24.04 vagy újabb, Debian 13). Telepítés rendszergazdaként: sudo apt-get install ./linvpn_X.Y.Z_<arch>.deb. A frissítéseket ugyanazok az offline Ed25519 aláírások védik (linvpn_X.Y.Z_<arch>.deb.sig); a LinVPN a frissítést a rendszer jelszókérésével (pkexec) telepíti. / LinVPN: linvpn_X.Y.Z_amd64.deb and linvpn_X.Y.Z_arm64.deb for Debian and Ubuntu based desktops (Ubuntu 24.04 or newer, Debian 13). Install as an administrator: sudo apt-get install ./linvpn_X.Y.Z_<arch>.deb. Updates are protected by the same offline Ed25519 signatures (linvpn_X.Y.Z_<arch>.deb.sig); LinVPN installs them with the system's password prompt (pkexec)."
          fi
```

(`TITLE` must already hold the WinVPN part when both are set; build it with `+=` as shown.)

3. `scripts/test-sign-release.sh`'s provenance check (the `grep -A3 'uses: actions/attest-build-provenance@'` line): add `&& grep -A3 'uses: actions/attest-build-provenance@' '$WF' | grep -q 'dist/linvpn_\*\.deb$'`.

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Tests scripts/lin-smoke.sh without a VM: its arguments, and (LINVPN_REMOTE_PRINT=1, which scripts/lin-remote.sh
# honours) the remote scripts it would run, in order.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
SCRIPT="$PWD/scripts/lin-smoke.sh"

PASS=0; FAIL=0
ok()  { PASS=$((PASS+1)); echo "ok   - $1"; }
nok() { FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); echo "FAIL - $1"; }
check() { local desc="$1"; shift; if "$@"; then ok "$desc"; else nok "$desc"; fi; }
has() { grep -qF -- "$2" <<<"$1"; }
lacks() { ! grep -qF -- "$2" <<<"$1"; }
before() { local a b; a=$(grep -nF -- "$2" <<<"$1" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); b=$(grep -nF -- "$3" <<<"$1" | head -1 | cut -d: -f1); [ -n "$a" ] && [ -n "$b" ] && [ "$a" -lt "$b" ]; }

check "bash -n" bash -n "$SCRIPT"
if command -v shellcheck >/dev/null 2>&1; then check "shellcheck" shellcheck "$SCRIPT" "$0"; else echo "skip - shellcheck is not installed"; fi

print() { LINVPN_SSH=linvpn-dev@example.invalid LINVPN_REMOTE_DIR=linvpn-work-slot9 LINVPN_REMOTE_PRINT=1 "$SCRIPT" "$@" 2>&1; }

for bad in de 'en;rm'; do
  OUT="$(print "$bad" || true)"
  check "refuses '$bad' with the usage line" has "$OUT" "usage: scripts/lin-smoke.sh [en|hu]"
  check "refuses '$bad' before any remote command" lacks "$OUT" "smoke-manage.sh"
done
check "needs LINVPN_SSH" bash -c "! env -u LINVPN_SSH LINVPN_REMOTE_PRINT=1 '$SCRIPT' en 2>&1 | grep -q smoke-manage"

EN="$(print)"
HU="$(print hu)"
check "default language en" has "$EN" "SMOKE_LANG=en scripts/smoke-manage.sh"
check "hu" has "$HU" "SMOKE_LANG=hu scripts/smoke-manage.sh"
check "builds the debug app with the embedded UI" has "$EN" "cargo build --locked -p macvpn-app --features custom-protocol"
check "builds the UI first" has "$EN" "(cd app/ui && npm ci && npm run build) &&"
check "never a release build" lacks "$EN" "--release"
check "the run is a desktop job" has "$EN" "systemd-run --user"
check "the desktop session is checked" has "$EN" "loginctl show-user"
check "the build comes before the run" before "$EN" "cargo build" "systemd-run --user"
check "never sudo, never the installed app" bash -c "! grep -qE 'sudo|/usr/bin/linvpn' <<<'$EN'"

echo "$PASS passed, $FAIL failed"
[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]
```

Run: `chmod +x scripts/test-lin-smoke.sh && scripts/test-lin-smoke.sh` → FAIL.

- [ ] **Step 2: `scripts/lin-smoke.sh`**

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# The Linux smoke run in linvpn-dev's desktop session (LinVPN plan, Task 23), started from the Mac: builds the debug
# app with the embedded UI on the VM (a plain job), then runs scripts/smoke-manage.sh as a `desktop` job of
# scripts/lin-remote.sh (systemd-run --user in the logged-in desktop session, which may be locked) and prints its log;
# the exit code is the run's. The run uses the debug app's sandbox and fakes only: never the real keyring, the
# installed LinVPN or a real connection.
#
#   scripts/lin-smoke.sh [en|hu]
#
# Environment: LINVPN_SSH and LINVPN_REMOTE_DIR as for scripts/lin-remote.sh; LINVPN_REMOTE_PRINT=1 prints the remote
# scripts instead of running them (scripts/test-lin-smoke.sh); LINVPN_SMOKE_WAIT: seconds for the run (default 1800).
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

usage() { echo "usage: scripts/lin-smoke.sh [en|hu]" >&2; exit 1; }
[ $# -le 1 ] || usage
LANG_ARG="${1:-en}"
[[ "$LANG_ARG" =~ ^(en|hu)$ ]] || usage
[ -n "${LINVPN_SSH:-}" ] || { echo "error: set LINVPN_SSH=linvpn-dev@<VM address>" >&2; exit 1; }
WAIT="${LINVPN_SMOKE_WAIT:-1800}"
[[ "$WAIT" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || { echo "error: LINVPN_SMOKE_WAIT: seconds" >&2; exit 1; }

scripts/lin-remote.sh run '(cd app/ui && npm ci && npm run build) && cargo build --locked -p macvpn-app --features custom-protocol'
LINVPN_JOB_WAIT="$WAIT" scripts/lin-remote.sh desktop "SMOKE_LANG=$LANG_ARG scripts/smoke-manage.sh"
```

Run: `chmod +x scripts/lin-smoke.sh && scripts/test-lin-smoke.sh` → PASS.

  *) SMOKE_OS=macos ;;
esac
```

# A file's permission bits: BSD stat on macOS, GNU stat on Linux.
mode_of() { if [ "$SMOKE_OS" = linux ]; then stat -c %a "$1"; else stat -f %Lp "$1"; fi; }
```

| the Dock, the AppKit windows, the activation policy (`if [ "$SMOKE_OS" = macos ]`) | unchanged (macOS only): Linux prints nothing for them; the driver logs "skipped on Linux" |
| `stat -f %Lp` | `mode_of` |

| a `windows` branch about RAS, the WinVPN Service, the phonebook, `WM_QUERYENDSESSION` | not Linux (the macOS side, or skipped if AppKit) |

- [ ] **Step 4: The driver** — `app/src-tauri/src/platform/smoke/linux.rs`:

```rust
//! The smoke run's Linux parts: the stand-in System Status report (every kind available through the stand-in
//! helper: L-b's NetworkManager engines do not exist yet), the skipped AppKit steps, and logout as SIGTERM to this
//! process (`macvpn_platform_linux::smoke::send_terminate`), which the platform's GLib signal source hands to the
//! terminate handler.

use macvpn_platform_api::SetupReport;

pub fn report(_ready: bool) -> SetupReport {
    SetupReport {
        package_manager: Some("apt".into()),
        sudo_works: true,
        installed_root_version: Some("smoke".into()),
        bundled_root_version: Some("smoke".into()),
        ..SetupReport::default()
    }
}

pub fn skipped(step: &str) {
    eprintln!("MacVPN smoke: {step}: skipped on Linux");
}

pub fn logout() {
    macvpn_platform_linux::smoke::send_terminate();
}
```

In `smoke/mod.rs`, for each `#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]` / `#[cfg(windows)]` pair (`grep -n 'cfg' app/src-tauri/src/platform/smoke/mod.rs`), add the Linux side:
- `mod linux;` under `#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]`;
- the helpers `private_temp_dir`, `mode`, `with_shot`, `shoot` that `macos.rs` implements with plain Unix calls: move them to a new `smoke/unix.rs` under `#[cfg(unix)]` (macOS keeps them byte for byte through that module); AppKit-only ones stay in `macos.rs` and Linux uses the Windows no-op shape (`with_shot` returns the source, `shoot` does nothing);
- `setup_report` / the ready report → `linux::report(false)` / `linux::report(true)`;
- the `dock`, `menu_shape`, screenshot and appearance-print steps → `linux::skipped("dock")` etc.;
- the system quit step (Windows' `query_end_session`) → `linux::logout()`, then the same checks of the log the macOS path makes;
- `update_install` → the Windows branch's sandbox behaviour (no installer in the sandbox);
- the control endpoint: Linux checks the socket file exists while on and is gone after (as macOS) *and* the endpoint string (as Windows): `state.control.endpoint()` equals `<sandbox>/run/linvpn/control/linvpn.sock`.

Every new `cfg` is inside `app/src-tauri/src/platform/` (allowed).

- [ ] **Step 2: The user-guide stubs** — `docs/linvpn-user-guide.en.md`:

```markdown
# LinVPN user guide

LinVPN is the Linux version of MacVPN and WinVPN: the same vault, connections, team catalogs and control API, for
Debian and Ubuntu based desktops (tested on Ubuntu with GNOME).

> **This is a preview build.** It creates and opens the vault, imports, edits, exports and backs up connections,
> subscribes to team catalogs and pairs apps, but it does not connect yet: connecting arrives with NetworkManager
> support in a later version. System Status says so.

## Installing

Download `linvpn_<version>_amd64.deb` (or `_arm64.deb`) from the release page and install it as an administrator:

    sudo apt-get install ./linvpn_<version>_amd64.deb

LinVPN appears in the app grid. Its icon sits at the right of the top bar (Ubuntu shows it through its
AppIndicator support; on GNOME without it, install the "AppIndicator and KStatusNotifierItem Support" extension).

## Where LinVPN keeps your data

- the vault and the connections: `~/.local/share/LinVPN`
- the settings: `~/.config/LinVPN`
- the vault's auto-unlock key: your login keyring (GNOME's Passwords and Keys), as "LinVPN vault key"

Auto-unlock protects the vault against other users and a stolen disk; it does not protect against software that
runs as you (any app of yours can ask your keyring). Turn it off in Settings if that matters to you.

## Updates

LinVPN checks for updates and installs a signed update after you approve it with an administrator's password. It
installs only a package signed by the maintainer, for your computer's architecture, and never an older version.

## Removing LinVPN

    sudo apt-get remove linvpn

Your data stays. To remove it too: delete `~/.local/share/LinVPN`, `~/.config/LinVPN`, `~/.cache/LinVPN`,
`~/.config/autostart/linvpn.desktop`, and the "LinVPN vault key" item in Passwords and Keys.
```

`docs/linvpn-user-guide.hu.md`: the same in Hungarian (the wording of the existing `docs/user-guide.hu.md` and `docs/winvpn-user-guide.hu.md` for the shared terms: széf, kapcsolat, csapatkatalógus, mesterjelszó; "bejelentkezési kulcstartó", "felső sáv", "Jelszavak és kulcsok").

Expected: all PASS.

- [ ] **Step 5: CP-update (tell the maintainer exactly this, then wait)** — the coordinator first builds a throwaway 2.0.6 package in a scratch worktree that is never committed (`Cargo.toml`, `app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`, `app/ui/package.json` set to 2.0.6), with `LINVPN_REMOTE_DIR=linvpn-work-cp scripts/lin-remote.sh run 'scripts/build-deb.sh'`, and fetches `dist/linux/linvpn_2.0.6_<arch>.deb` to the Mac's `target/cp-update/`.

> **CP-update.** On the Mac, sign the throwaway test package with the release key (dry run: nothing is uploaded):
>
> ```bash
> scripts/sign-release.sh --dry-run --deb target/cp-update/linvpn_2.0.6_<arch>.deb --arch <arch> v2.0.6
> ```
>
> Give the coordinator the `signature: …` line. Then at the VM's desktop, logged in as `linvpn-dev`, in a terminal
> (the coordinator has put both files into `~/.cache/LinVPN/updates/`, a folder only `linvpn-dev` can read):
>
> ```bash
> pkexec /usr/libexec/linvpn/linvpn-install-update --deb "$HOME/.cache/LinVPN/updates/linvpn_2.0.6_<arch>.deb" \
>   --sig "$HOME/.cache/LinVPN/updates/linvpn_2.0.6_<arch>.deb.sig" --version 2.0.6 --asset linvpn_2.0.6_<arch>.deb; echo "exit $?"
> dpkg-query --show linvpn
> ```
>
> The system asks for an administrator's password ("LinVPN wants to install its update."). Expected: `exit 0`, then
> `linvpn 2.0.6`. Run the same `pkexec` line once more: expected `exit 17` (not newer). Finally return to 2.0.5 and
> delete the test files:
>
> ```bash
> sudo apt-get install --allow-downgrades /home/linvpn-dev/linvpn-work-slot1/dist/linux/linvpn_2.0.5_<arch>.deb
> rm -f "$HOME/.cache/LinVPN/updates/linvpn_2.0.6_"*
> ```
>
> and on the Mac: `rm -rf target/cp-update`.

Before the maintainer's run, the coordinator also proves the refusal: a copy of the package with one byte appended, the same `.sig`, `--deb` pointing at it → the maintainer sees `exit 13` (bad signature) and nothing installed (add that line to the instructions above when sending them).
